**[00:08:52]** If you had asked me a few years ago

**[00:08:54]** about the Bible,

**[00:08:56]** I would probably have told you it was either made up to control the masses

**[00:09:06]** but I hope to inspire you

**[00:09:08]** to see it in a different light

**[00:09:10]** because it truly is so much more.

**[00:35:18]** wanted, a being that was capable

**[00:35:20]** of willingly engaging in a loving

**[00:35:22]** relationship with Him,

**[00:35:24]** He had to allow for the inherent risk

**[00:35:26]** that this being would utterly reject Him.

**[00:35:28]** Not only that,

**[00:35:30]** but since God is the source of all

**[00:35:32]** life, what this further means

**[00:35:34]** is that He must have been

**[00:35:36]** prepared for His creation to

**[00:35:38]** woefully destroy itself by rejecting

**[00:35:40]** this source of life and where it came from.

**[00:35:46]** When considering the timeline of creation,

**[00:35:48]** and all possibilities that could happen

**[00:35:50]** as this freedom would play itself out,

**[00:35:52]** God, being the perfectly moral

**[00:35:54]** and just being that He is,

**[00:35:56]** chose the most effective way

**[00:35:58]** for it all to unfold.

**[00:36:00]** The way that would cause the least

**[00:36:02]** amount of collateral damage.

**[00:36:04]** The way that would lead us collectively

**[00:36:06]** back to the truth and back to the

**[00:36:08]** original design in the fastest

**[00:36:10]** amount of time.

**[00:36:12]** Welcome to our present universe.

**[00:36:18]** But what happened at the very

**[00:36:20]** beginning? And why,

**[00:36:22]** if given the entire world in a

**[00:36:24]** perfect state, as well as

**[00:36:26]** the presence of our very own Creator for

**[00:36:28]** guidance, why would man

**[00:36:30]** disobey the one and only rule

**[00:36:32]** and damn himself into a reality

**[00:36:34]** of suffering, struggle,

**[00:36:36]** and death?

**[00:36:38]** Why would God make a rule

**[00:36:40]** regarding knowledge in the first place?

**[00:36:42]** And why is this story

**[00:36:44]** so relevant to us today?

**[00:36:46]** Stay with me,

**[00:36:48]** my friend, because as we travel

**[00:36:50]** deeper and deeper down this rabbit hole,

**[00:36:52]** it will all make sense in ways

**[00:36:54]** that I promise will change your life

**[00:36:56]** forever.

**[00:37:00]** I believe this story is

**[00:37:02]** more relevant to us today than at any

**[00:37:04]** other time, and in this journey

**[00:37:06]** you will learn exactly why.

**[00:37:08]** But first,

**[00:37:10]** we have to do a little more digging.

**[00:37:12]** We know

**[00:37:14]** that our Creator is loving, and can

**[00:37:16]** only be motivated by the truth.

**[00:37:18]** What this means is that

**[00:37:20]** in creating us, He wanted an authentic

**[00:37:22]** relationship where we would

**[00:37:24]** come to Him willingly.

**[00:37:26]** Not out of fear of His power, or

**[00:37:28]** because we wanted His approval,

**[00:37:30]** but because we knew why we loved

**[00:37:32]** Him as our Creator and genuinely

**[00:37:34]** desired a relationship.

**[00:37:36]** This is why we have a

**[00:37:38]** conscious conscience, why we are

**[00:37:40]** told to love one another and to honor

**[00:37:42]** our parents. It is why we are

**[00:37:44]** fundamentally driven by truth in

**[00:37:46]** what we want from the day we are born

**[00:37:48]** as little explorers of this magical realm.

**[00:37:52]** We also know that our Creator

**[00:37:54]** declared the end from the beginning,

**[00:37:56]** and what this means is that He chose

**[00:37:58]** the most moral and effective option

**[00:38:00]** possible when considering our timeline,

**[00:38:02]** the problem of free will,

**[00:38:04]** and how it would all play out so

**[00:38:06]** that we would be saved from ourselves and

**[00:38:08]** be back with Him in the end.

**[00:38:10]** But there are so many questions,

**[00:38:12]** and I can feel them with you right now,

**[00:38:14]** because I too have asked myself

**[00:38:16]** these questions many times.

**[00:38:18]** Why did man reject God?

**[00:38:20]** And if God is so forgiving,

**[00:38:22]** why did He curse man by kicking him

**[00:38:24]** out of paradise after a simple mistake?

**[00:38:26]** Why is there so much suffering

**[00:38:28]** in the world if God is loving

**[00:38:30]** and forgiving?

**[00:38:32]** Why is there so much suffering

**[00:38:34]** in the world if God is loving

**[00:38:36]** and forgiving?

**[00:38:38]** Why hasn't God stepped in to fix any of it

**[00:38:40]** or to prove His existence once and for all?

**[00:38:42]** Why are there evil people in the world

**[00:38:44]** who are doing whatever they want with it?

**[00:38:46]** Why do children and animals

**[00:38:48]** get abused every single day?

**[00:38:50]** Why are there wars and poverty

**[00:38:52]** and sickness?

**[00:38:54]** Why?

**[00:38:56]** These are some of the big questions

**[00:38:58]** of life, aren't they?

**[00:39:00]** They are questions that few

**[00:39:02]** people can answer.

**[00:39:04]** But they are not the only

**[00:39:06]** questions of life.

**[00:39:08]** They are questions that few

**[00:39:10]** dare to ask and give an honest

**[00:39:12]** exploration of, because what they

**[00:39:14]** lead to is unsettling and

**[00:39:16]** genuinely uncomfortable.

**[00:39:18]** It is much easier to blame God

**[00:39:20]** than to say He is nowhere to be found.

**[00:39:22]** To buy into the illusion

**[00:39:24]** that this is all there is,

**[00:39:26]** that we are the ones in charge

**[00:39:28]** of our brief little lives

**[00:39:30]** in this magical realm.

**[00:39:32]** Yet these answers don't explain away

**[00:39:34]** these uncomfortable questions, do they?

**[00:39:36]** We need something greater than

**[00:39:38]** ourselves.

**[00:39:40]** And truly, if your heart is open,

**[00:39:42]** you know that your spirit yearns

**[00:39:44]** for something greater.

**[00:40:04]** Stay with me, friend,

**[00:40:06]** because as hard as these questions

**[00:40:08]** may be, there are answers

**[00:40:10]** we will find, and should you choose

**[00:40:12]** to accept them, they will lead you

**[00:40:14]** to spiritual liberation.

**[00:40:18]** The truth indeed shall set us free.

**[00:40:20]** And so, it is with this

**[00:40:22]** timeless maxim in our mind that we

**[00:40:24]** must now press onward and discover

**[00:40:26]** the truth about what happened in

**[00:40:28]** the garden, and why.

**[02:54:20]** and it is the uncomfortable part that I warned you about.

**[02:54:30]** and you have to see clearly again.

**[02:54:42]** it's all full of lies,

**[02:54:44]** half-truths,

**[02:54:46]** and outright fabrications designed to keep you enslaved.

**[02:54:48]** and outright fabrications designed to keep you enslaved.

**[02:54:52]** is this one right here.

**[02:54:56]** and the author of your life.

**[02:55:00]** marketed to,

**[02:55:04]** and profited from.

**[02:55:10]** and return to our relationship with God

**[02:55:18]** just as they choose to do,

**[02:55:20]** all the while ignorant of the

**[02:55:32]** It's time to wake up.

**[02:55:34]** It's time to wake up.

**[02:55:36]** You and I, and everyone we care about

**[02:55:38]** are on a sinking ship.

**[02:55:40]** But that sinking ship

**[02:55:42]** is an orchestra playing.

**[02:55:44]** And people are so amused and entertained

**[02:55:46]** by the lovely music,

**[02:55:48]** so much that they are oblivious

**[02:55:50]** to what is happening.

**[02:55:54]** Even though the water is slowly rising

**[02:55:56]** past their ankles,

**[02:55:58]** past their knees and waist,

**[02:56:00]** and up to their necks,

**[02:56:02]** they still prefer to talk about what they did,

**[02:56:04]** what they'll do,

**[02:56:06]** what they like and who they don't like,

**[02:56:08]** or what new things they'll buy and consume.

**[02:56:10]** Meanwhile, they have no clue

**[02:56:12]** that they're about to drown.

**[02:56:24]** It was the same for the Titanic.

**[02:56:26]** On that fateful night,

**[02:56:28]** people had a good time

**[02:56:30]** and talked about the normal things

**[02:56:32]** anyone tends to talk about.

**[02:56:34]** People were dancing and listening to music

**[02:56:36]** and eating their favorite meals.

**[02:56:40]** And yet, it wasn't a normal night

**[02:56:42]** but a new one.

**[02:56:44]** This is the world we find ourselves in today.

**[02:56:46]** And when you wake up from the matrix,

**[02:56:48]** it's not just about waking up to a new paradigm.

**[02:56:50]** It is realizing that the world around you

**[02:56:52]** is on a collision course

**[02:56:54]** with a giant iceberg.

**[02:56:56]** There will come a day

**[02:56:58]** when this world will sink

**[02:57:00]** and be forgotten forever.

**[02:57:02]** And those who have found the lifeboat

**[02:57:04]** will sail to freedom.

**[02:57:06]** While those who refuse to wake up from the illusion

**[02:57:08]** will unfortunately drown.

**[02:57:10]** The time to wake up is now.

**[02:57:12]** And what that means first and foremost

**[02:57:14]** is changing your paradigm

**[02:57:16]** so that you can see clearly again.

**[02:57:18]** We are not the center of our universe

**[02:57:20]** and we are not in control.

**[02:57:22]** This is a fact.

**[02:57:24]** But can we still have free will

**[02:57:26]** in this new paradigm?

**[02:57:28]** Are we just fancy robots

**[02:57:30]** doomed to execute a predetermined choreography?

**[02:57:32]** Do we lose specialness

**[02:57:34]** and uniqueness in any of these things?

**[02:57:36]** Or do we lose our own identity

**[02:57:38]** and our own identity?

**[02:57:40]** How can we change any way

**[02:57:42]** by sacrificing this egotistical view of freedom?

**[02:57:44]** And how do we explain all of the unexplainable evils

**[02:57:46]** and misfortunes in the world

**[02:57:48]** and those that befall us every single day?

**[02:57:54]** So many questions.

**[02:57:56]** So much anxiety.

**[02:58:02]** Stay with me, friend.

**[02:58:04]** Because it's about to make sense very soon.

**[02:58:08]** Because we are stuck in time and space

**[02:58:10]** and it is impossible to be free

**[02:58:12]** in the way that our self-awareness

**[02:58:14]** fools us to be through its many illusions.

**[02:58:16]** The only being that can choose

**[02:58:18]** whatever he wants is God.

**[02:58:20]** Because God is outside of time and space

**[02:58:22]** and without cause or end.

**[02:58:24]** This part is over our head

**[02:58:26]** but we need to understand this

**[02:58:28]** for the next point.

**[02:58:30]** Tell me, can God choose

**[02:58:32]** anything that he wants?

**[02:58:34]** Yes, of course.

**[02:58:36]** But this too has a caveat.

**[02:58:38]** God is incapable of lying

**[02:58:40]** and morally perfect.

**[02:58:42]** So therefore, even his spectrum of choices

**[02:58:44]** is limited.

**[02:58:46]** He can only choose what is the morally best option.

**[02:58:48]** And therefore, we can trust

**[02:58:50]** that his choice to bring about time and space

**[02:58:52]** in the way that it's unfolding right now

**[02:58:54]** must have been the best possible way.

**[02:58:58]** With this in mind,

**[02:59:00]** we must now change our paradigm

**[02:59:02]** of what it means to choose.

**[02:59:08]** Choosing from the illusion of self

**[02:59:10]** works on a spectrum of different options

**[02:59:12]** that we exercise autonomously.

**[02:59:14]** But this is an illusion.

**[02:59:18]** Only God can do this.

**[02:59:20]** Nothing is truly independent

**[02:59:22]** and we have no control over anything that happens.

**[02:59:24]** We merely interpret it

**[02:59:26]** and assign a meaning.

**[02:59:28]** We are not the gods of our world

**[02:59:30]** but rather observers and participants.

**[02:59:36]** But yet, we are

**[02:59:38]** self-aware and sadly,

**[02:59:40]** one of the best examples of this free will

**[02:59:42]** is suicide.

**[02:59:48]** We are the only being on earth

**[02:59:52]** And this willful rejection of life

**[02:59:54]** is the utmost example

**[02:59:56]** of our self-awareness program

**[02:59:58]** running amok in our lives

**[03:00:00]** and its ultimate cost.

**[03:00:18]** but rather

**[03:00:20]** a binary black and white option.

**[03:00:22]** Free will is not being able to make

**[03:00:24]** any choice you want at any time

**[03:00:26]** but rather being able to make

**[03:00:28]** THE choice between two simple paradigms.

**[03:00:32]** One is the illusion of self

**[03:00:34]** or the forbidden fruit

**[03:00:36]** and the other is the truth

**[03:00:38]** that God is the center of the universe

**[03:00:40]** and the author of everything that happens

**[03:00:42]** and consequently

**[03:00:44]** we are his servants.

**[03:00:46]** One is a paradigm of humility

**[03:00:48]** where our identity

**[03:00:50]** is an extension of the creator

**[03:00:52]** and in service to creation

**[03:00:54]** constantly listening and communicating

**[03:00:56]** and allowing his divine plan to unfold

**[03:00:58]** through the vessel we call our body

**[03:01:00]** mind and spirit

**[03:01:02]** or as we Christians call

**[03:01:04]** the temple of the Holy Spirit.

**[03:01:06]** This is why the Bible tells us

**[03:01:08]** we are made in the image of God

**[03:01:10]** and why Jesus was the perfect image.

**[03:01:12]** Our choice to align

**[03:01:14]** with this created purpose

**[03:01:16]** is what gives us peace, abundance

**[03:01:18]** and freedom from the slavery of ego.

**[03:01:24]** But this is very difficult

**[03:01:26]** and requires a virtue that is in short supply today.

**[03:01:28]** Humility.

**[03:01:36]** It is much easier to operate in the default paradigm of pride

**[03:01:38]** where our identity is an extension of our self-awareness

**[03:01:40]** fooling itself into replacing God

**[03:01:42]** as the center of the universe

**[03:01:44]** and the author of creation.

**[03:01:46]** It is much easier to feel as though

**[03:01:48]** we are the God of our life

**[03:01:50]** to worship ourselves

**[03:01:52]** the material world

**[03:01:54]** other gurus, nature

**[03:01:56]** or anything other than who actually created us.

**[03:02:00]** But why?

**[03:02:04]** The answer is

**[03:02:06]** because giving back the throne to God

**[03:02:08]** means we are accountable to the purpose

**[03:02:10]** he intended for us.

**[03:02:12]** It means we must be moral

**[03:02:14]** and driven by truth and obedience

**[03:02:16]** because he is perfectly moral

**[03:02:18]** and the source of all truth.

**[03:02:20]** Put simply,

**[03:02:22]** it is hard.

**[03:02:24]** And this is why the big fat lie of free will

**[03:02:26]** was so easy, delicious

**[03:02:28]** and intoxicating for Adam and Eve.

**[03:02:30]** What a fruit indeed

**[03:02:32]** because it cost them

**[03:02:34]** and us an unimaginable paradise.

**[03:02:36]** It is in choosing this illusion

**[03:02:38]** that Adam and Eve disconnected themselves from God

**[03:02:40]** and damned humanity with the ego.

**[03:02:42]** And it is by seeing it

**[03:02:44]** for what it truly is

**[03:02:46]** an illusion

**[03:02:48]** that we can return to our proper place

**[03:02:50]** saved from what is soon going to happen

**[03:02:52]** and able to live forever

**[03:02:54]** in harmony with our creator

**[03:02:56]** as it was always intended.

**[03:03:06]** The only thing we can do

**[03:03:08]** is to be faithful to God

**[03:03:10]** and to love him

**[03:03:12]** and to love our God
